Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The physical operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 993. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 550 with 4 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to physical structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 993/550 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 212.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The physical variety V defined by structural decomposition constraints admits a stratification into 290 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 499 and intersection form of rank 4.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to physical theory, we decompose the problem into 290 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 499. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(4/n). QED.
